bc:T721,{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"FAQPage","mainEntity":[{"@type":"Question","name":"Is AwaitIO an Interval alternative?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Yes. It uses the same model: an action is an async function, and every await io.input call shows a form and waits. The UI is served by your own app instead of a hosted service."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Is Interval still available?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Not as a hosted product. Meter acquired Interval in November 2023, and interval.com redirects to meter.com.
